Earlier — 2026-08-11 - Completed quick-519: fixed the template chooser's clipped subtitle, horizontal scrollbar and crowded cards. **Two of the three defects were one bug, and not where the brief guessed** (it expected an unconstrained chip row or a card min-width): `DialogContent` is a **grid** with an implicit `auto` column, whose minimum is the max of its items' min-content widths — and a `truncate`d chip's min-content is its whole string, since `truncate` sets `white-space: nowrap`. `max-w-full` does not help, because **percentage max-widths are ignored during intrinsic sizing**. So one 54-char facility name widened the track past `max-w-lg`, which produced the scrollbar (`overflow-y-auto` forces `overflow-x` to `auto`) AND the clipped subtitle (`DialogHeader` is a sibling grid item stretched to the same over-wide track, so the description wrapped wider than the visible box). The subtitle itself was never broken and was not edited. Fix is one class, `grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)]`; the shared `ui/dialog.tsx`/`ui/badge.tsx` primitives were deliberately left alone. Chip gained `min-w-0` + `title` for hover; density moved onto 8/12/16/20/24 on both surfaces with a hairline divider above "No template". **Caught in review, missed by the executor:** mobile's candidate-name `Text` had `numberOfLines={1}` but no `flexShrink` — RN defaults `flexShrink` to 0 unlike web, so a long name still pushed the sheet past 360pt. Visual only; strings byte-identical. **Gate probed twice and blind the first time** — not `.next/dev/types` but 10 untracked files carrying literal stray tool-output text plus 4 invalid `readonly Array<T>`; a **parse** error in any file suppresses semantic checking of the entire program, a broader rule than CLAUDE.md currently records. Earlier — 2026-08-10 - Completed quick-518: fixed the ghost-stop promotion quick-517 deferred. **517 was wrong in both halves of that deferral** — the path IS reachable (apply A, press Change, pick B, through the chooser 516 had just built; 517 inferred otherwise from one component's conditional) and it was confirmed on screen; its characterisation test made a known-wrong behaviour look settled and is now an assertion. Applying B after A promoted A's skipped not-on-manifest row into a real stop (unskipped, badged New, Linked, counted in "matched", nothing to deliver) because `buildTemplateDiff` ignored `skipped` and `mergeTemplateStop` clears it on matched rows. Signal used: `templateOrigin === 'TEMPLATE_ONLY'` AND `skipped` AND no document backing — a conjunction, so every doubt KEEPS the row (a kept ghost, a ghost with typed freight, a skipped document stop, a legacy row are all untouchable). Fix is ONE filter in `buildTemplateDiff`: a ghost can't be matched, isn't appended, and — since the merge walks `diff.rows` — is dropped with its provenance link, then re-derived by the incoming template (listed → re-inserted skipped; not listed → gone). Same template twice is now idempotent, and the preview stays honest since both are built from that function. `reDerived` reported in the dialog and the post-apply line. Earlier — task 491: Fixed the FreightMap SSR hydration mismatch that showed as the red "1 Issue" Next.js dev badge on both the sign-in and sign-up auth screens (surfaced while verifying the task-490 signup reskin). Root cause: components/auth/freight-map.tsx renders US state outlines using d3-geo's geoAlbersUsa projection, which is trigonometric (Math.sin/cos/sqrt); those transcendental functions produce last-ULP-different results between Node's V8 (server-side render) and the browser's V8 (client), so the server-rendered SVG path `d` coordinate strings didn't byte-for-byte match the client's on hydration → React hydration mismatch warning. Because FreightMap is a shared component used by both SignInCard and the new SignUpCard, both auth pages showed it. Fix: render the decorative <svg> CLIENT-ONLY — added a `mounted` boolean (useEffect(() => setMounted(true), [])) and gate the <svg> behind it; the outer container <div ref={containerRef}> still server-renders so the resize/getBoundingClientRect measurement keeps working, but the mismatching SVG paths are never emitted during SSR (server + first client render both render just the empty container → they match, then the map mounts after hydration). No visible regression since the map is purely decorative (aria-hidden, role=presentation) and already fades in via its entrance animation. Verified: npx tsc --noEmit = 0 errors; and a Playwright console check at 1280px on both pages reported 0 hydration errors (previously 1) while the map still renders (64 state-outline paths present after mount on each). This also removes the badge from the pre-existing sign-in screen, not just signup. Not deployed, not pushed. Earlier — task 490: Reskinned the "Start your free trial" signup wizard to match the dark, branded Sign in screen. The user pointed out (while testing at 400px) that the signup form — a light shadcn card — didn't match the mobile design system; it also didn't match its sibling auth page (Sign in), which is a dark, branded two-panel design. Confirmed the target via AskUserQuestion (options: match Sign in / use the ds mobile kit / point at a reference) → user chose "Match the Sign in screen", so the design source of truth is components/auth/sign-in-card.tsx (dark #0E172A outer / #0E1424 form panel, DriveCommand mono-light mark + "Miles Ahead." tagline, Input tone="dark" (#070B14 fields), uppercase text-label text-n-300 labels, gradient b-500→b-600 primary button, b-300 links, brand-critical error style, animated FreightMap + RotatingTips on desktop). Implementation: (1) NEW components/auth/sign-up-card.tsx — a two-panel shell that mirrors SignInCard's layout, reusing the existing FreightMap (memoized) + RotatingTips for the desktop left panel, the DriveCommand brand block, the mobile-only branding block (shown when the left panel is hidden at <md), the "Start your free trial" heading + "No credit card required. 14-day trial included." subtitle, and <SignUpForm> in the right panel; framer-motion entrance animations guarded by useReducedMotion. (2) sign-up-form.tsx fully restyled to the dark tokens WITHOUT touching any wizard logic — tone="dark" on every Input, uppercase n-300 plain <label>s (dropped the shadcn Label import), a b-500 progress bar on a #1C2536 track with white/n-500 step labels, gradient primary buttons (Continue with an ArrowRight icon + Create free account via the existing SubmitButton/useFormStatus), a dark ghost "Back" button, a dark #070B14 <select> for "How did you hear about us?", brand-critical error blocks (dropped the shadcn Alert import), and a b-300 "Sign in" link. (3) components/ui/password-input.tsx is now tone-aware: it forwards `tone` to the underlying Input and picks the eye-toggle contrast (text-n-400/hover:text-n-200 on dark, muted-foreground default) — the light default is preserved so the component stays reusable elsewhere. (4) sign-up/[[...sign-up]]/page.tsx now just renders <SignUpCard searchParams={…}> (removed the old light card wrapper + inline heading, which moved into SignUpCard). The 2-step wizard behavior (progress, per-step client validation, value preservation across Back/Continue, only-final-submit), all fields (incl. the numeric truck count from 488 and the optional heard-about from 489), and signUpAction/provisionTenant are UNCHANGED — this was a pure restyle. Verification: npx tsc --noEmit = 0 errors; and Playwright screenshots against the live dev server confirmed the match at BOTH breakpoints — 390px renders a single dark branded column (brand block, heading, blue progress bar, uppercase labels, dark inputs, eye toggle, gradient "Continue →" button, blue "Sign in" link; step 2 shows the dark select + "Create free account" + ghost "Back"), and 1280px renders the full two-panel (left: DriveCommand brand + animated freight map with CHI/DAL route arcs + rotating TIP card; right: the dark wizard) — visually matching Sign in. One diagnostic note: an early 700ms screenshot caught the form mid entrance-animation and looked empty, but a console/DOM check confirmed the form is present + visible (firstName in DOM, visible) once the ~0.8s fade completes; and the red "1 Issue" Next.js dev badge is a PRE-EXISTING FreightMap SVG float-rounding hydration mismatch (server vs client path coordinates) that the Sign in screen shares — NOT introduced by this task (could be fixed separately by rounding the generated path coords deterministically). Not deployed, not pushed. Earlier — task 489: Added an optional "How did you hear about us?" acquisition-channel field to the tenant signup wizard's final step (step 2, placed under Promo code), stored it on the Tenant record, and surfaced it on the SysAdmin tenant detail page. Added two nullable TEXT columns Tenant.heardAbout + Tenant.heardAboutOther via raw-SQL migration 20260722000003_add_tenant_heard_about (ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS; the auto-deploy hook did not fire so applied+verified on the remote Supabase DB via MCP apply_migration; npx prisma generate synced the generated client). Created a shared module src/lib/onboarding/heard-about.ts exporting HEARD_ABOUT_OPTIONS ([google_search:Google search, referral:Referral, social_media:Social media, industry_event:Industry event, other:Other]) + heardAboutLabel(value), imported by BOTH the client signup form and the server SysAdmin page so the stored key and its display label can never drift. Form (sign-up-form.tsx): a native <select name="heardAbout"> with a blank "Select an option…" default option; selecting "Other" reveals a free-text <Input name="heardAboutOther" maxLength={200}> — the reveal is driven by a new heardAbout useState (the select is controlled just for the conditional; it still submits via its name). The field is labeled "(optional)". Strictly optional and non-blocking: the schema fields are heardAbout/heardAboutOther z.string().max().optional() (deliberately loose — not an enum — so an unexpected value can never fail account creation), and leaving it empty submits with no validation error. The signup action reads both from FormData (|| undefined when empty) and adds heardAbout to the tenant.created AppEvent; provisionTenant stores heardAbout + heardAboutOther on Tenant.create, keeping the free text ONLY when heardAbout==='other' (stray text for the fixed options is dropped to null). SysAdmin surfacing: getTenantById (app/(admin)/actions/tenants.ts) now selects heardAbout, heardAboutOther, and truckCount, and tenants/[id]/page.tsx renders a new "Acquisition" block in the Tenant Info card showing "Heard about us: {label}" plus the quoted free text for Other (or "Not provided"), and "Trucks at signup: N". Verification: npx tsc --noEmit = 0 errors; and a REAL end-to-end Playwright run at 390px did two signups against a freshly-restarted dev server — (A) acquisition left EMPTY → redirected to /onboarding/welcome and stored heardAbout=null/heardAboutOther=null (confirming it never blocks), and (B) selected "Other" and typed free text → the free-text input revealed, signup completed, and the Tenant stored heardAbout='other', heardAboutOther='Found via a trucking podcast ad'. The admin round-trip was verified at the data layer (the stored columns are exactly what getTenantById selects and what the page JSX renders via heardAboutLabel), but the authenticated SysAdmin browser render was not driven (no sysadmin credentials), so the "other" test tenant a802496e-deb3-414f-982f-28c209f2832c was intentionally LEFT in the DB so it can be eyeballed live at localhost:3000/tenants/a802496e-deb3-414f-982f-28c209f2832c (the local dev server has the admin-page change; prod does not until deploy) — it is pending deletion via the same safe child-first procedure once confirmed. The "empty" test tenant (672a0c1c) and its 4 auth users were deleted and verified gone. Reused the lesson from task 488: used ABSOLUTE paths for all file tools this time (no stray nested tree), and pre-emptively restarted the :3000 dev server after prisma generate so it loaded the fresh client (the stale-client "Unknown argument" failure mode from 488 was avoided on the second run but still hit once before the restart). Not deployed, not pushed. Earlier — task 488: Replaced the "Start your free trial" signup form's banded "Fleet size" dropdown with a numeric "Number of trucks" input, deriving the segmentation band server-side from the number and storing BOTH the exact count and the derived band (implemented the PRIMARY option, not the minimal-change alternative). Added a nullable Tenant.truckCount Int? column (camelCase, no @map, matching fleetSizeBucket) via raw-SQL migration 20260722000002_add_tenant_truck_count (ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS; the auto-deploy hook did not fire so it was applied + verified on the remote Supabase DB via MCP apply_migration; npx prisma generate synced the generated client). In onboarding.schemas.ts, added deriveFleetSizeBucket(truckCount) mapping 1-3→OWNER_OPERATOR, 4-15→SMALL, 16-50→MEDIUM, 50+→LARGE (upper bound inclusive, so 50→MEDIUM and 51→LARGE), and a truckCount field z.coerce.number().int('Enter a whole number').min(1,'Enter at least 1 truck').max(100000). Kept fleetSizeBucket in the schema (derived + injected by the action) deliberately so signUpSchema stays a ZodObject — the 2-step wizard from task 487 uses signUpSchema.pick({firstName,lastName,email,password}) for step-1 validation and a top-level .transform would have broken .pick. The signup server action (actions.tsx) reads truckCount from FormData, derives the band server-side (with a defensive fallback so an invalid count surfaces the error on truckCount rather than a phantom fleetSizeBucket field, and never reaches provisioning), injects fleetSizeBucket into the parsed payload, and adds truckCount to the tenant.created AppEvent properties. provisionTenant stores truckCount on Tenant.create alongside fleetSizeBucket. Backward-compat: Tenant.fleetSizeBucket is still populated (now derived), so every existing reader — admin tenant pages, hydrate-tenant, seed-sample-data — keeps working unchanged. The form's step 2 now renders <Input type="number" inputMode="numeric" min={1} step={1} required name="truckCount"> ("Number of trucks") replacing the <select>. Added tests/unit/onboarding/derive-fleet-size-bucket.test.ts (4 boundary tests). Verification: npx tsc --noEmit = 0 errors; vitest = 4/4 pass; and a REAL end-to-end signup via Playwright at 390px with truckCount=20 confirmed the numeric field replaced the dropdown (type=number/inputmode=numeric/min=1, legacy #fleetSizeBucket count=0), the submit redirected to /onboarding/welcome, and the stored Tenant row had truckCount=20 AND fleetSizeBucket=MEDIUM (exact count kept + band derived correctly). The throwaway test tenant and its 4 auth users were then deleted and verified gone (0 rows). Two incidents worth noting: (1) the first live signup attempt failed with a Prisma "Unknown argument truckCount" because the long-running dev server on :3000 held a STALE Prisma client generated before this task's schema change (the action's rollback correctly deleted the orphan auth user) — resolved by restarting the dev server so it loaded the freshly generated client, after which the signup succeeded; the user's :3000 dev server was restarted and left running. (2) Several relative-path Write/Edit calls made while the shell cwd was apps/web resolved against that cwd and created a STRAY nested apps/web/apps/web/ copy of onboarding.schemas.ts + the test file (while the REAL onboarding.schemas.ts stayed unedited, which is why tsc initially reported deriveFleetSizeBucket/truckCount as missing) — detected via a duplicate-file find, deleted the entire stray apps/web/apps tree, and re-applied both files to their real locations using absolute paths; final git tree verified clean with no stray. Lesson: use absolute paths for file tools since the shell cwd drifts to apps/web across turns. Not deployed, not pushed. Earlier — task 487: Refactored the "Start your free trial" tenant signup form (apps/web/src/app/(auth)/sign-up/sign-up-form.tsx) from a single long screen into a 2-step wizard with a progress indicator, built primarily for mobile Safari. Step 1 "About you" = First name, Last name, Work email, Password (with the show/hide eye toggle added in the earlier password-toggle task); Step 2 "Your company" = Company name, Fleet size, Promo code (optional). Exactly the same fields — no new required fields added. The core technique: keep ONE `<form action={signUpAction}>` and render BOTH step field-groups always-mounted, toggled purely with the Tailwind `hidden` class (step === 1 ? 'space-y-4' : 'hidden'). Because the inputs stay uncontrolled and never unmount, (a) entered values persist across Back/Continue for free (no controlled state, no hidden mirror inputs) and (b) the FormData submitted on the final step is byte-identical to the pre-refactor single-screen form — so signUpAction, provisionTenant, and onboarding.schemas.ts are completely untouched (contract preserved). Only step 2 renders a submit button (the existing SubmitButton/"Create free account" with useFormStatus), so nothing POSTs to the server until the final step; step 1's footer is a type="button" "Continue", step 2 adds a ghost "Back". Enter on step 1 is intercepted via onKeyDown to call Continue (there's no submit button there to implicitly submit). Per-step validation: step1Schema = signUpSchema.pick({firstName,lastName,email,password}) reuses the EXACT server Zod rules (names min 1, email valid, password min 8) so client and server never disagree — validateStep1() reads new FormData(formRef.current), safeParses, sets inline errors, focuses the first invalid field, and blocks advancing; step 2 keeps native required + the unchanged server-side validation (fieldErrors). Native required on the now-hidden step-1 fields is safe because the client gate guarantees they're filled before they're hidden, so they can never be "invalid + unfocusable" at submit. A useEffect on the action state jumps back to step 1 if the server ever returns a step-1 fieldError so it stays visible. Progress indicator = two flex-1 rounded bars (bg-primary when filled, bg-muted otherwise) + "About you"/"Your company" labels (active = text-foreground) + "Step X of 2" + role="progressbar"; Tailwind + shadcn tokens only, no new dependency; full-width inputs/buttons and type=email/autoComplete hints for iOS keyboards + autofill. Verification: npx tsc --noEmit = 0 errors; and a real end-to-end run at 390px via Playwright against the already-running local dev server (:3000, which serves the edited file via HMR) passed 22/22 checks — step 1 shown first with the company field hidden; empty Continue blocked with no POST; invalid email + short password blocked; valid step 1 advances to step 2 (company visible, step-1 fields hidden); Back preserves First name + email; forward preserves Company name; NO POST during navigation; and EXACTLY ONE POST on the final "Create free account" submit whose payload contains firstName, lastName, email, password, companyName, and fleetSizeBucket. Screenshots at 390px confirm both steps render in the shadcn card with the progress bar and no horizontal overflow. To avoid outward-facing side effects, the final POST was intercepted/aborted in the test so NO real tenant, auth user, or confirmation email was created in the shared Supabase — the server action + provisioning flow are unchanged and receive identical FormData, so real end-to-end provisioning is unaffected (a live run with a throwaway email was flagged rather than performed). A stray :3457 dev-server attempt deferred to the existing :3000 instance and exited; the user's server was left untouched. Not deployed, not pushed. Earlier — task 486: Added a first-run navigational coach-mark tour for new owner/dispatcher users, built for mobile Safari (390px). It's a hand-rolled spotlight overlay (NO new dependency) mounted inside OwnerShell; because the (owner) route group is OWNER/MANAGER-only, mounting there automatically scopes the tour to the owner/dispatcher portal and excludes the driver and sysadmin portals. Five skippable steps — intro (centered) → the + create menu → the bottom tabs (Trips/Loads/Dashboard/Live Map/More) → the Get started checklist → the Help (?) icon. The overlay dims the screen, rings the current target with a white spotlight (box-shadow: 0 0 0 9999px), and shows a coach-mark card (step counter, Back/Next/Done, always-present Skip); the dim backdrop is tap-through (advances), it renders at z-[2000] above the header (z-[1001]) and bottom nav (z-50), and portals to document.body. Key architecture finding driving the design: the +menu/tabs/help chrome exists ONLY inside OwnerShell, while the "Get started" activation checklist (ActivationChecklist) renders ONLY on the standalone /onboarding/welcome page (no chrome). So the tour runs in the shell and its "checklist" step targets the in-shell OnboardingReminderRibbon (the persistent nudge to the checklist); steps whose target isn't present at runtime (the ribbon on an activated tenant, or all mobile targets on desktop) are filtered out at start, so it self-adjusts to 4–5 visible steps. Persistence: new per-user boolean User.onboardingTourSeen (plain camelCase, @default(false), matching isSample/isDispatchReady) via raw-SQL migration 20260722000001_add_user_onboarding_tour_seen (idempotent ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS, no per-column grants needed); the auto-deploy hook did NOT fire in this session so the column was applied + verified on the remote Supabase DB via Supabase MCP apply_migration, and `npx prisma generate` synced the second generated schema copy at src/generated/prisma so user.update({data:{onboardingTourSeen}}) typechecks. (owner)/layout.tsx reads the flag with $queryRaw (fail-safe to true on error — a nagging tour is worse than a missing one) and passes tourSeen to OwnerShell, which wraps its tree in OnboardingTourProvider and tags the mobile-branch chrome with data-tour="create"/"help"/"checklist" (wrappers in owner-shell.tsx) and "tabs" (on the <nav> in owner-bottom-nav.tsx) — only the mobile branch is tagged to avoid duplicate matches with the CSS-hidden desktop branch, and resolveTarget picks the visible (non-zero-rect) element. markTourSeen server action mirrors markCongratsShown (requireAuth() → getTenantPrisma().user.update, own row, never throws). "Replay walkthrough" (a ReplayTourButton with a PlayCircle icon) was added to the Help Center page /help (the "?" affordance) and restarts the tour from step 1 via the provider's start() from context; per the "resets and replays" requirement, replay re-runs client-side and the persisted flag governs only the one-time automatic first-run (documented interpretation). Auto-start fires exactly once, mobile-only (matchMedia max-width:1023px), 600ms after mount so the sticky header/bottom nav are laid out before measuring, and is guarded by a ref + a sessionStorage flag (covers the race where a user skips then navigates before markTourSeen commits) + the persisted DB flag. Never blocks interaction: Skip is always present, backdrop tap advances, and the tour dismisses on skip/done. Verification: `npx tsc --noEmit` = 0 errors (none in touched files); the remote onboardingTourSeen column confirmed present via information_schema; and the 390px render was ACTUALLY EXECUTED — a Playwright run (chromium, viewport 390×844) against a faithful static harness that ports the overlay's exact positioning math (SPOTLIGHT_PAD=6, CARD_GAP=12, placeAbove=rect.top>vh/2, left-4 right-4 mx-auto max-w-md) with real data-tour chrome elements: all five steps produce a card spanning x:16–374 inside the 390px viewport with NO horizontal overflow (docW==winW==390), the intro centered, header-target cards just below the header, and the tabs card correctly placed ABOVE the bottom nav (top 590 of 844); screenshots visually confirmed the spotlight rings the +, the bottom tab bar (which stays bright while the rest dims), the reminder ribbon, and the ? — all legible and within the viewport. Recommended final check: open /carrier/dashboard as a brand-new owner in mobile Safari / DevTools at 390px against live chrome, and tap Replay walkthrough on /help. No new npm dependency; not deployed, not pushed. Earlier — task 485: Brought the driver-portal invite controls (items 3 & 4 of quick-484) to the mobile-web DS driver detail (DriverDetailMobile), closing the follow-up flagged in 484. Previously the phone-width DS detail showed only an "Invited" status pill and offered no way to send or resend the invite — the send/resend/status controls existed only on the desktop half (PortalAccessControls). Added a "Portal Access" card to the Profile tab that mirrors the desktop behavior: a status line ("Invite not sent" / "Invite sent {date}" / "Portal active" / "Access suspended") with a DS StatusPill (Not invited / Invited / Active / Suspended), and a full-width DS PrimaryButton labeled "Send invite" (state none) or "Resend invite" (state pending) shown only when canManageAccess is true and disabled with an inline hint when the driver has no email on file. The button posts to the UNCHANGED POST /api/v1/carrier/fleet/drivers/{id}/resend-invitation (resendCarrierDriverInvitation handles both first-send and resend), with the same toast handling as desktop (alreadyProvisioned→info, warning→warning, else success) followed by router.refresh(). New DriverPortalAccess interface + computePortalState() helper mirror desktop PortalAccessControls.computeAccessState (active/suspended/pending/none). Key correctness detail: the portal facts (userId/userIsActive/invitationStatus/invitationSentAt) are threaded as a SEPARATE `portal` prop and read directly from props — deliberately NOT folded into the component's local `data` state, which is seeded once via useState(driver) and would go stale after router.refresh() — so the status line + date update immediately after sending. page.tsx passes the new `portal` object + canManageAccess (both already computed for the desktop half in 484). Scope kept tight: Revoke/Restore remain desktop-only, and there are NO API/route/lib/email/accept-invitation changes. tsc 0 errors (none in the 2 touched files: DriverDetailMobile.tsx, [id]/page.tsx). Earlier — task 484: Made the driver-portal invite email opt-in. Creating a carrier driver used to auto-send the invite whenever an email was present, with no way to opt out — wrong for paperwork-pending/seasonal hires. Backend (lib/carrier/fleet-drivers.ts createCarrierDriver + api/v1/carrier/fleet/drivers POST): the DriverInvitation-create + sendDriverInvitation call is now gated on a new sendInvite flag — `if (email && sendInvite === true)` — with sendInvite destructured out of the Prisma create payload so it's never persisted as a column; the v1 POST Zod schema accepts sendInvite?: boolean. Since createCarrierDriver's only caller is that one route and all create surfaces are updated here, defaulting sendInvite to off (omitted → not sent) is the intended product change. Forms: added a "Send portal invite email now" control, default UNCHECKED, to both New Driver forms — desktop CarrierDriverForm (shadcn Checkbox, rendered create-mode only, sends sendInvite only when !isEdit) and mobile-web DS DriverCreateMobile (DS Toggle inside a new "Portal Access" section with "Off by default. You can send it later from the driver's profile." caption). InviteDriverSheet — the dedicated "Invite Driver" quick-sheet whose whole purpose is inviting — now explicitly posts sendInvite:true so its behavior is preserved under the new default (no checkbox there). Detail screen (items 3 & 4) built on the existing desktop PortalAccessControls, which already had Grant/Resend/Revoke/Restore wired to the unchanged resend-invitation route: relabeled the state=none button "Send invite" and state=pending "Resend invite", and rewrote the status line to "Invite not sent" / "Invite sent {date}" / "Portal active" / "Access suspended". The sent date comes from a new getLatestInvitationInfo(orgId, email) helper (returns {status, sentAt:createdAt}) threaded page.tsx → DriverDetailActions → PortalAccessControls as invitationSentAt; success toasts reworded to "Invite sent/resent to {email}". Did NOT touch the invite email template/content or the accept-invitation flow, and left existing field validation unchanged (sendInvite only gates whether the invite is sent). Added tests/unit/carrier/create-driver-invite-optin.test.ts (3 tests: sendInvite false and omitted send no email + create no DriverInvitation; sendInvite true does both). tsc 0 errors (none in the 9 touched files); new vitest 3/3 pass. Explicit follow-up: the mobile-web DS driver detail (DriverDetailMobile) still shows only an "Invited" pill and has no send/resend buttons — desktop detail fully satisfies items 3 & 4, extending them to the DS view is deferred. Earlier — task 483: Wired inline New Facility creation into the mobile-web New Load STOPS section so users no longer hit a dead end. Previously MobileStopsEditor (used by NewLoadMobile) showed "No facilities yet. Stops happen at facilities. Add one first, then come back." with no way forward, forcing users to leave a half-filled load. The desktop flow already embeds the shared FacilityForm inline (StopBuilderAddModal via its onSuccess/onCancel props); this brings the same to mobile. Changes (single file, components/carrier/stops/MobileStopsEditor.tsx): reuse the existing FacilityForm (no duplication) inside a DS SheetContainer rendered after — so layered above — the Add Stop sheet; open it from two entry points — an "Add new facility" PrimaryButton on the STOPS empty state (dead-end copy replaced with "Stops happen at facilities. Add your first one right here.") and a "+ Add new facility" option in the stop-facility picker (sentinel value intercepted in the select onChange). On successful save, handleFacilityCreated appends the new facility to a local createdFacilities list (merged over the server-provided facilities prop via allFacilities so it's instantly pickable), auto-selects it for the stop being added (form.facilityId), closes the create modal, and reopens the Add Stop sheet. The in-progress load survives the round trip: NewLoadMobile owns every load field (clientId/contractId/commodity/rate/stops/etc.) in useState and the create path only POSTs to /api/v1/carrier/facilities + updates child state — no navigation, no router.refresh, no remount (router.push only fires on final load submit). Works both with zero facilities (empty-state button) and when adding an additional stop to a load that already has stops (picker option); MobileStopsEditor is shared so route templates get the same inline-create. Data model, APIs, and the rest of the New Load form unchanged. tsc clean; desktop New Load already had inline create so left as-is. Earlier — task 482: Improved new-tenant onboarding so users stop stalling on an empty Trips screen. (1) The "Get started checklist" (app/onboarding/welcome/checklist.tsx) now follows the real data dependency chain in order — Add your first client → Create a contract for that client → Create your first load → Assign the load to a trip and dispatch — each row deep-linking to the matching /carrier/{clients,contracts,loads,trips}/new form. Each step is marked complete when the underlying record actually EXISTS (not on click): new getOnboardingFlags(tenantId) in page.tsx counts clients/contracts/loads/trips, excluding sample (isSample) and soft-deleted (deletedAt) rows so seeded demo data never falsely completes a step; the progress % is computed in the component (account + 4 steps), and 100% keeps the existing "You're all set!" view. Wired into all 3 render paths (normal, hydrate-catch, unauthenticated preview). (2) The Trips overview mobile-web empty state (TripsMobile.tsx) replaced the dead-end "No trips yet. Tap + to plan your first trip." with guided states: when the tenant has no real load → "Create a load first" + a primary "New load" button to /carrier/loads/new; when loads exist but no trips → "Assign a load to start a trip" + an "Assign a load" button to /carrier/trips/new. trips/page.tsx passes hasLoads (real non-sample non-deleted load count > 0). Manager (read-only) + filtered-empty states unchanged. Trip creation flow and data model were NOT changed (reads/counts + one prop only); copy kept short/jargon-free; tsc clean. Desktop DispatchesGrid keeps the generic data-grid empty state (the quoted copy was the mobile-web view). Earlier — task 481: Fixed 2 bugs in the "New Dispatch Assigned" email (sending path: trips.ts createDispatch/updateDispatch -> after() -> sendDispatchAssignedNotification in lib/carrier/notifications.ts -> DispatchAssignedEmail template). Bug 1 (Total Stops showed 0): the payload counted trip._count.stops, but stops belong to the assigned LOAD (carrier_stops.load_id, reached via trip.carrierLoads[].stops[]) — recomputed Total Stops from the load stops and added the itinerary (sequence, type, facility name, city) to the email body; stops flatten across loads and sort by the dispatch-wide unique sequenceOrder, re-numbered 1..N. Bug 2 (Scheduled Departure ~5h off): rendered via toLocaleString with NO timeZone so it printed in the server zone (UTC) — "1:00 PM" for an 8:00 AM CDT trip; now rendered in the tenant timezone (Tenant.timezone) with timeZoneName:'short' -> "Jul 19, 2026, 8:00 AM CDT". DB storage of departure is UNCHANGED (still the absolute UTC instant). Payload logic extracted to a pure, unit-testable module lib/carrier/dispatch-assigned-email.ts; added 1 test file (4 tests: stopCount 2, ordered itinerary, 8:00 AM CDT in both built payload + rendered HTML). tsc clean. Follow-up noted: load-delivered/stop-completed/client notification templates share the same no-timeZone datetime pattern. Earlier — task 480: Fixed the blocking driver-portal invite flow. A login is a globally-unique Supabase Auth user (auth.users.email is unique across ALL tenants) mapped 1:1 to a Prisma User, but invite acceptance only pre-checked the tenant-scoped Prisma User table — so an invited email that already had any Supabase identity (e.g. an owner/driver in another tenant) made admin.createUser throw "email already registered", which the route swallowed into a generic "An error occurred while creating your account" 500 that repeated identically on every retry (verified against prod: ayazali7244@gmail.com is an OWNER of tenant 9ce1797e but was invited as a driver to 04305834; RLS ruled out by reproducing the User insert). Fixes: accept-invitation now returns a SPECIFIC 409 when createUser reports email-exists (not the generic 500); createCarrierDriver normalizes email on write so the acceptance-time carrier_drivers.userId link (keyed on the lowercased email) can't silently fail; resendCarrierDriverInvitation is now idempotent — a driver with a linked login or an ACCEPTED-but-unlinked driver returns { alreadyProvisioned } (self-healing the userId link), and the resend route + PortalAccessControls surface it as an info state ("This driver already has portal access") instead of a red error toast. Added 2 unit test files (5 tests, all pass); tsc clean. Owner/sysadmin login + signup untouched. Earlier — task 479: Sample/demo drivers (User.isSample=true) are now excluded from the New/Edit Route driver + co-driver pickers — listDrivers() gained an excludeSamples option and both route pages pass { activeOnly: true, excludeSamples: true }, so only real active accepted drivers are selectable; the edit page's already-assigned-driver merge is left unfiltered so an existing assignment to a sample/deactivated driver is never silently dropped. Earlier — task 478: New Route primary + co-driver pickers now list only ACTIVE drivers (User.isActive=true) — listDrivers() gained an optional { activeOnly } param; create + edit route pages pass it; the edit page preserves an already-assigned but since-deactivated driver by re-fetching + merging it so existing assignments aren't silently dropped. Drivers-management and tags pages unchanged. Earlier same day — task 477: Fixed New Route save regression (routes weren't saving) — the New Route driver picker was sourcing carrier fleet drivers whose userId often isn't a DRIVER-role User, breaking createRoute's Route.driverId->User(role=DRIVER) contract; re-sourced primary + co-driver pickers from listDrivers() (DRIVER-role Users) on desktop + mobile, create + edit. Also redesigned the form: origin/destination/stops are now ONE ordered waypoint list (first=Pickup/origin, last=Delivery/destination, middle=stops) reusing FacilityAddressSelect, with origin/destination/stops_*/distanceMiles derived from array position so the createRoute/updateRoute FormData contract stays unchanged. Prior task 476 added the facility dropdowns (tkt-0078,0079).
